Ruling: refusal representation; project retained. The only references the project's expansion has beyond the second's are scope gates the project carries as refusals on Steps 1 and 2 (scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income), which the transcription reports as gates rather than judging per line; the Step 1 to 5 tests, including the Step 4 birth-date question eic.born_after_1960, appear on both sides. The project's first test (AGI below the largest Step 1 limit) is implied by Step 1, question 1.

Second transcription's note: Each STOP that says to check 27c counts only when it is reached in the flow; earlier steps must pass. The nonresident-alien STOP is refused (scope.other_income). The filer's own choice not to claim the EIC is not asked.

Ruling: representation and scope; project retained; equivalence not proved. The project's expansion differs only by the same scope gates as eic.credit; the line 27c instructions are written in the second's own structure. The project counts each STOP that says to check line 27c only where the flow chart reaches it, plus Worksheet A line 2 zero; ten Stage 9 fixtures assert it, each predicted by hand.

Second transcription's note: The EIC Table has columns by filing status and by number of children (0, 1, 2, 3). The grammar's table_lookup takes only key and status, so each child column is a separate pinned table (eic_table_0 to eic_table_3). The '3' column is used for 3 or more.

Ruling: representation; project retained. Both choose the EIC Table column by the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs; the project adds the guard for an earned income below the table's first row ($1), which has no printed row (ambiguity register amb.2025.eic_below_table).

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The same choice (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount; the smaller of lines 5 and 2 otherwise) written as two guards instead of nested choices.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.other_claimant, eic.clergy, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | dependent.divorced_rule, eic.clergy |
| eic.check_27c | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.other_claimant, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | dependent.divorced_rule |
| eic.separated_box | dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.other_claimant, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | dependent.divorced_rule |
| eic.clergy | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 1) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.schedule1 | scope.schedule1 |
| (Step 5, 3) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 1 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 2 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 4 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Clergy and church employees must reduce this amount by income also on Schedule SE. Schedule SE is outside the tool, so this line refuses on scope.schedule1 and otherwise copies line 1z."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule 1 line 8s amounts are outside the tool and are refused through scope.schedule1, so this line is zero."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 3)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 1)", take: "eic.line(Step 5, 2)"}

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "The election is not computed. Any nontaxable combat pay is refused, so this line is zero."

  - id: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["eic.line(Step 5, 3)", "eic.line(Step 5, 4)"]}

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "eic.line(Step 5, 5)"}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs as defined earlier. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, then: eic.h_l2_c0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, then: eic.h_l2_c1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, then: eic.h_l2_c2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, then: eic.h_l2_c3}
    note: "The EIC Table has columns by filing status and by number of children (0, 1, 2, 3). The grammar's table_lookup takes only key and status, so each child column is a separate pinned table (eic_table_0 to eic_table_3). The '3' column is used for 3 or more."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, then: eic.h_l5_c0}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, then: eic.h_l5_c1}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, then: eic.h_l5_c2}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, then: eic.h_l5_c3}
    note: "The worksheet leaves line 5 blank when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the Part 2 threshold. This line always computes the lookup, and line 6 uses it only in the case where the worksheet fills in line 5."

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_smaller

  # ---------- helpers: basic values ----------
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_mfs_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household (Step 3 question 3; Step 4 question 1)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.married, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_hoh, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_children
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (whether or not they have a valid SSN)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule]
    note: "The tie-breaker rules for a qualifying child of more than one person, the married-child rule and the divorced-parent rule are not computed and are refused."

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Per Step 3 and Schedule EIC, a qualifying child without a valid SSN still routes the filer through Step 3, skipping Step 4. That child is not counted in eic.h_children, which picks the table column."

  # ---------- helpers: income limits (Step 1 q1, Step 5 q3) ----------
  - {id: eic.h_limit0_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$19,104", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit0_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$26,214", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit1_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$50,434", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit1_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$57,554", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit2_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$57,310", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit2_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$64,430", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit3_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$61,555", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_limit3_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$68,675", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for the number of children with valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit1_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit1_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit2_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 2}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit2_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit3_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 3}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_limit3_joint}

  # ---------- Step 1 ----------
  - id: eic.h_s1_agi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: Is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_ssn
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have a social security number issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return (including extensions) that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.taxpayer_work_ssn, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income]
    note: "No spouse SSN input is available, so this tests the taxpayer only, using household.taxpayer_work_ssn as the EIC-valid SSN. Step 1 question 3 (Form 2555) and question 4 (nonresident alien) are refused through sa.excluded_income and scope.other_income."

  # ---------- Step 2 ----------
  - id: eic.h_cg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 line 7a; if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [f1040.line7a, eic.h_zero]}

  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_cg]}

  - id: eic.h_inv_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$11,950"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.investment_limit}

  - id: eic.h_inv_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2: Investment income not more than $11,950 (otherwise STOP unless filing Form 4797)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, eic.h_inv_limit]}
    refuses: [sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16]
    note: "Form 4797 (question 3) and Schedule E, rental, Form 8814 and passive activity income (question 4) are refused rather than computed."

  - id: eic.h_pre3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed (reach Step 3)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1_agi, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_inv_ok, {const: true}]}]}

  # ---------- Step 3 ----------
  - id: eic.h_q45
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, questions 4 and 5 passed: did not share a principal residence for the last 6 months, or legally separated and living apart at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed (filers with a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_q45, {const: true}]}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  # ---------- Step 4 ----------
  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "The text allows the spouse's age on a joint return. No spouse input exists, so only the taxpayer's answers are tested."

  - id: eic.h_s4_pass
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (filers without a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}]}
    note: "Step 4 question 6 (can be claimed as a dependent) uses sa.dependent, which states the same not-required-to-file exception."

  - id: eic.h_path_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed with a qualifying child, or Step 4 passed without one"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_pass, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_pass, {const: true}]}]}

  # ---------- Step 5 ----------
  - id: eic.h_s5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3: Is your earned income less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: ["eic.line(Step 5, 5)", eic.h_limit]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Step 5 questions 1 and 2 (clergy, church employee, self-employment, statutory employee; Worksheet B) are refused through scope.schedule1. Step 6 (IRS figures the credit) is not asked; the tool uses Worksheet A."

  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 to 5 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre3, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_path_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s5, {const: true}]}]}

  # ---------- Table lookups ----------
  - {id: eic.h_l2_c0, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 1 amount, no children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l2_c1, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 1 amount, one child column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l2_c2, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 1 amount, two children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l2_c3, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 1 amount, three children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l5_c0, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 3 amount, no children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l5_c1, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 3 amount, one child column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l5_c2, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 3 amount, two children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}
  - {id: eic.h_l5_c3, printed: "(helper)", label: "EIC Table, line 3 amount, three children column", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_table_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}}

  # ---------- Worksheet A Part 2 threshold ----------
  - {id: eic.h_ws0_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$10,620", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_ws0_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$17,730", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}}
  - {id: eic.h_ws1_other, printed: "(helper)", label: "$23,350", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}}
  - {id: eic.h_ws1_joint, printed: "(helper)", label: "$30,470", type: NonNegDollars, expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}}

  - id: eic.h_ws_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for the number of qualifying children with valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws0_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_children, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws0_joint}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws1_other}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_children, {const: 1}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}, then: eic.h_ws1_joint}

  - id: eic.h_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5: Is the amount on line 3 less than the threshold?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_ws_threshold]}

  - id: eic.h_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  # ---------- Required intermediate values ----------
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit for Form 1040, line 27a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}, then: eic.line6}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}, then: eic.h_zero}
    refuses: [eic.clergy, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16, s8812.combat_pay]

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (instructed by a STOP, or Worksheet A line 2 is zero)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s1_agi, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn, {const: false}]}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_q45, {const: true}]}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}]}
    note: "Each STOP that says to check 27c counts only when it is reached in the flow; earlier steps must pass. The nonresident-alien STOP is refused (scope.other_income). The filer's own choice not to claim the EIC is not asked."

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_pre3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_q45, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Read literally, the Step 3 question 6 'No' box tells every No answerer to check the box. Following the specification and the Special rule for separated spouses, this is limited to married MFS or HOH filers who passed questions 4 and 5."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 27b: a minister, member of a religious order, or Christian Science practitioner filing Schedule SE with an amount on Schedule SE line 2 also reported on Form 1040, line 1z"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [scope.schedule1, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule SE, including clergy income, is covered by scope.schedule1, which is refused. So whenever this line computes, it is false."
```
```
